Consulting the Table of &chi;&sup2; Critical Values and a level of significance &alpha;=0.05, we obtain a critical value of 7.81.</p><p>Since the chi-squared value of 0.79 is less than the critical value of 7.81, the null hypothesis has FAILED TO BE REJECTED.</p><hr/> <p>Your lab partner completed a chi-squared (&chi;&sup2;) test on your lab data (above) for the F<sub>2</sub> generation in a standard dihybrid cross. The goal was to verify if the observed results matched the expected phenotype ratios.</p><p>However, it appears they made an error. <strong>What did they do wrong?</strong></p>	The numbers in the calculation have to be squared and they are not squared.
